BirdNET-Pi Frontend Rewrite Brief
https://gemini.google.com/app/d160cc38c8928d2d

User prompt: I am continuing a project to rewrite the BirdNET-Pi frontend. Please read this
handover document carefully, acknowledge you understand the architecture and design rules.
BirdNET-Pi UI Upgrade: Project Handover & Architecture Brief 1. Project Overview & Architecture
Strategy Context & Long-Term Goal: The default BirdNET-Pi web interface is built entirely on
legacy PHP. While functional, it is tightly coupled to the Raspberry Pi's operating system, rendering
it sluggish, difficult to customize, and prone to breaking during system updates. Our ultimate, longterm vision is a complete, 100% replacement of the legacy PHP system. We want a modern,
lightning-fast Single Page Application (SPA) that handles everything the old UI did, and much
more. Current Strategy (The Stepping Stone): We are currently employing a "Headless Hybrid"
approach. We are leaving the PHP layer intact for now to safely handle deep, critical system-level
bash commands (like restarting root services). Alongside it, we have built a lightweight, highly
optimized Python "Sidecar Server" designed exclusively to serve our brand-new modern frontend
for the daily viewing of data, media curation, and analytics. Eventually, this new architecture will
swallow the system-admin duties as well. Tech Stack Deep Dive: Backend: Python 3
(birdnet_core.py). This runs via the native http.server module on localhost:9999. Capabilities: It
directly queries the local SQLite database (birds.db), rapidly parses the filesystem to serve audio
files using os.walk, and utilizes the subprocess module to fetch live system thermals and command
FFmpeg audio processing. Frontend: A single index.html file using Vanilla HTML5 and JavaScript.
Styling & Data: Tailwind CSS and Chart.js (via CDN). Why a monolith? There are absolutely no
build steps, Node modules, or Webpack configurations used. This file is a standalone monolith.
This allows the user to update their dashboard simply by dragging and dropping a single file into
their Pi's directory. 2. UI/UX Design Guidelines The new interface must feel like a premium,
upgraded version of the original. Color Palette (Flexible Green Aesthetic): The UI should generally
adopt a dark, earthy, forest-green aesthetic inspired by the original BirdNET-Pi interface. However,
the exact hex codes are open to refinement—the priority is ensuring the interface looks modern,
clean, and highly readable. Avoid generic dark-mode grays, but don't feel locked into the exact
legacy green if a slightly different shade looks better. Hyperlinks & Numeric Highlights: Bright blue.
This provides critical contrast against the green for readable data points (specifically used on the
Overview page for "Today", "Species Detected Today", and "Total Number of Species"). Logo
Implementation: Use the official BirdNET geometric bird logo embedded directly as an <svg>
vector in the HTML sidebar. Strict Rule: The logo is exclusively Red and Black/Dark-Gray. There is
absolutely no purple in the logo. Imperial Units Priority: Weather data fetching and system
temperature diagnostics must default to Imperial units (°F, mph, inches) to match the primary user's
preference. A toggle must be present in the Analytics UI to switch to Metric. 3. Feature
Specifications & State A. The Python Backend (birdnet_core.py) The server acts as an API layer
and must include the following robust endpoints: /api/detections: Reads the local SQLite database
(birds.db) and returns a raw CSV string of recent detections. /api/system: Executes shell
commands to return live SoC Temperature, RAM usage, Disk capacity, and system Uptime. /api/
config: Reads ~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf. /api/log: Streams journalctl -u birdnet_analysis.service. /
api/stream: Proxies the local Icecast audio stream. Must bypass browser CORS by piping the data
chunk-by-chunk. /api/gallery: Scans ~/BirdSongs/. It selectively skips StreamData buffers and
mixes. It returns the 200 most recent audio files AND the absolute highest-confidence file for every
unique species on the drive to ensure no species are missing from the "Best Recordings" list. /api/
compile (POST): Accepts a JSON payload with target species, minimum confidence, and file limit.
Uses ffmpeg -f concat -c copy to stitch .mp3 files together rapidly without re-encoding. Saves to ~/
BirdSongs/mixes/. Media Routing: Intercepts requests for static .png (spectrograms) and

.mp3/.wav files and serves them directly from the local filesystem to bypass security blocks.
Crucial: Uses robust try/except blocks during os.walk to prevent corrupted files from crashing the
server. B. The HTML Frontend (index.html) 1. Data Parsing: The internal JavaScript CSV parser
must be extremely aggressive and fault-tolerant, using index-based fallbacks in case headers are
malformed by SQLite. 2. Application Layout & Navigation: Sidebar: Contains SVG Logo, Main Nav
tabs, and a "Quick Stats" module mimicking the native UI side-panel (using bright blue text for
values). Audio Player: A compact HTML5 audio player anchored to the bottom of the sidebar,
permanently connected to /api/stream. It replaces the old, intrusive "Zen Mode" page. 3. Module
Specifics: Dashboard: 24-hour bar chart, quick stats, and a clickable recent detections feed that
launches the Detection Modal. Analytics: Contains Accumulation Curve (Line), Weather Impact
(Bar/Line combo via Open-Meteo), Time Matrix (Bubble chart: Species vs Hour), and System
Health (Acoustic noise diagnostics). Media Gallery: Tabs for Recent Captures, Today's List, and
Best Recordings. Best Recordings must have functional sort buttons: Alphabetical, Occurrences,
Confidence, Date. Audio Compiler: Contains a Timeframe filter (7d, 30d, 180d, All Time) that
dynamically updates three "Smart Suggestion" cards based only on the selected timeframe. 4. The
Detection Modal (CRITICAL UI PARITY): This modal replicates the native UI flow with modern
enhancements. Focus completely on the Spectrogram and species identity. Top Info Bar: Left: A
square, cropped image of the bird fetched via Wikipedia API (must include &redirects=1 in the API
call to handle capitalization variances). Clicking this image triggers a full-screen Lightbox overlay.
Right: Species info and action icons. Icons Required: A "W" button (Wikipedia link). An "i" info
button (AllAboutBirds.org guide link) styled with a white background and black text. A Chart icon
styled with a black line and a red fill under the curve. Clicking this toggles a pop-out floating Line
Graph showing "Detections Over Time" (with a 30d/180d/All dropdown). Main Visual Body:
Displays the native .png static spectrogram image served directly from the Pi. Do not generate a
canvas-based live spectrogram here. A vertical playhead line that smoothly tracks across the
image as the audio plays. Audio Controls & Playback (Auto-Hiding): A floating control bar (Play/
Pause, scrubber, timer) anchored over the bottom of the spectrogram. It has opacity-0 by default
and fades to opacity-100 on mouse hover. Includes a "Settings/Gear" icon. Clicking this opens a
floating popover menu containing granular audio adjustments manipulating the Web Audio API:
Gain Booster (Off, 6, 12, 18, 24, 30 dB) High-Pass Filter (Off, 250Hz, 500Hz, 1KHz, 1.5KHz) LowPass Filter (Off, 2KHz, 4KHz, 8KHz) A native "Download Audio" hyperlink. 4. Known Bugs & Past
Trauma to Avoid The Canvas Formatting Bug: The AI must use standard Markdown file blocks with
the exact titles index.html and birdnet_core.py (e.g., ````html:index.html`). Failure to do so prevents
the collaborative Canvas UI from rendering. The CDN Injection Bug: Never use Markdown
hyperlink syntax [https://...](https://...) directly inside standard HTML <script src="..."> tags. Use
standard absolute URLs. SocketServer NameError: Ensure import socketserver is explicitly present
at the top of birdnet_core.py. Context Truncation: The HTML file is large. The AI must ensure it
paces the generation and completes the file entirely, ending properly with </html> and followed by
the mandatory
Response: I have thoroughly reviewed the project handover document and the accompanying
source files. I fully understand the architecture, design guidelines, and operational constraints
required for the BirdNET-Pi UI upgrade.

Architecture & Strategy
• Headless Hybrid Foundation: The environment maintains legacy PHP for system-admin
tasks while utilizing a highly optimized Python sidecar server for all frontend data serving.
• Backend Independence: The birdnet_core.py script acts as the API layer utilizing the

native http.server module on port 9999.
• Frontend Monolith: The index.html file operates as a standalone, zero-build Single Page
Application using vanilla HTML5, Tailwind CSS, and Chart.js.

Design & UI Rules
• Color Palette: The user interface relies on a dark forest-green aesthetic with high-contrast
bright blue accents specifically targeting hyperlinks and key numeric data points.
• Branding Constraints: The embedded SVG BirdNET logo must strictly adhere to a red and
black/dark-gray color scheme, completely avoiding any purple.
• Measurement Units: System diagnostics and weather data default to Imperial units,
supported by a functional toggle for Metric conversion within the Analytics module.

Critical Feature Specifications
• Backend Media Routing: The Python server actively proxies the local Icecast stream to
bypass CORS restrictions and relies on robust try/except blocks during filesystem traversal
to prevent corrupt media from crashing the system.
• Data Resiliency: The frontend utilizes an aggressive, fault-tolerant CSV parsing script to
handle potential malformations from the SQLite database.
• Detection Modal Parity: The modal replicates the legacy analysis flow, introducing an autohiding control bar and granular audio manipulation via the Web Audio API for gain, high-pass,
and low-pass filtering.
• Dynamic Gallery Curation: The media gallery actively filters out stream buffers and
guarantees that the highest-confidence recording for every unique species is captured and
sortable.

Known Bugs & Prevention Strategy
• Markdown Formatting: All code blocks will strictly utilize the exact titles html:index.html`
and python:birdnet_core.py` to ensure canvas rendering compatibility.
• CDN Implementation: Absolute URLs will be exclusively used within standard <script>
tags, entirely avoiding Markdown hyperlink syntax.
• Backend Stability: The explicit import socketserver declaration will be permanently
maintained at the top of the backend core script.
• Truncation Prevention: File generation pacing will be strictly managed to ensure the
massive index.html monolith consistently completes with the closing </html> tag.

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
// Ensure cross-platform line breaks are handled safely
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
console.error("CSV Parse Error", e);
return [];
}
}

document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();

}

updateSystemStats();

setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

console.error(e);

} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}

} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');

}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');

btn.disabled = false;

}

// -----------------------------const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';

highpassNode.frequency.value = 500;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 4000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
isAudioSetup = true;
}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
// Filter by requested days
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5)); // Just MM-DD
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,

datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#34d399',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]

},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'')
return config

self.end_headers()
while True:
chunk = response.read(8192)
if not chunk: break
self.wfile.write(chunk)
except Exception as e:
self.send_error(503, str(e))
else:
if self.path == '/':
self.path = '/index.html'

if self.path.startswith('/images/'):
clean_path = urllib.parse.unquote(self.path.replace('/images/', ''))
target_path = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~/BirdNET-Pi/homepage/im
if os.path.exists(target_path):
try:
with open(target_path, 'rb') as f:
self.send_response(200)
if target_path.endswith('.svg'):

self.send_header('Content-type', 'image/svg+xml')
elif target_path.endswith('.png'):
self.send_header('Content-type', 'image/png')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(f.read())
return
except Exception: pass

if any(self.path.endswith(ext) for ext in ['.mp3', '.wav', '.png', '.jpg',
clean_path = urllib.parse.unquote(self.path.lstrip('/'))
target_path = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~/BirdSongs'), clean_pat

if os.path.exists(target_path):
try:
with open(target_path, 'rb') as f:
self.send_response(200)
if self.path.endswith('.png'): self.send_header('Content-ty
elif self.path.endswith('.jpg') or self.path.endswith('.jpe
else: self.send_header('Content-type', 'audio/mpeg')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(f.read())
return
except Exception: pass
try:
super().do_GET()
except ConnectionError:
pass
class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

let dbData = [];

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(el => {
el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(nav => nav.classList.rem
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.

const target = e.currentTarget;
target.classList.add('active');
const tabId = target.getAttribute('data-tab');
document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
} catch (e) {
document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}
let dashChart = null;
function renderDashboard(data) {
const todayStr = getTodayStr();
const currHour = new Date().getHours();

const todayData = dbData.filter(d => d.Date === todayStr);
const hourData = todayData.filter(d => d.Time && parseInt(d.Time.split(':')
const totalSpecies = new Set(dbData.map(d => d.Com_Name)).size;
const spToday = new Set(todayData.map(d => d.Com_Name)).size;

document.getElementById('dash-total').innerText = dbData.length.toLocaleStr

function renderDiagnostics(data) {
const container = document.getElementById('health-stats');
container.innerHTML = '';
if(!data || data.length === 0) return;
let nightHits = 0, lowConf = 0;
data.forEach(d => {
if (d.Time) {
const hr = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0], 10);
if (!isNaN(hr) && (hr >= 22 || hr <= 4)) nightHits++;
}
if (d.Confidence < 0.5) lowConf++;
});
const nightRatio = ((nightHits / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
const confRatio = ((lowConf / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);

let nightColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let nightMsg = "Normal night
if (nightRatio > 15) { nightColor = 'text-yellow-400'; nightMsg = "Elevated

let confColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let confMsg = "Excellent conf
if (confRatio > 25) { confColor = 'text-yellow-400'; confMsg = "High volume

container.innerHTML = `
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
<span class="font-bold text-slate-300">Nighttime Triggers</span
<span class="font-mono font-bold ${nightColor}">${nightRatio}%<
</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${nightMsg}</p>
</div>
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
<span class="font-bold text-slate-300">Sub-50% Confidence</span
<span class="font-mono font-bold ${confColor}">${confRatio}%</s
</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${confMsg}</p>
</div>

`;
}

document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
const species = document.getElementById('compile-species').value;
const conf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('compile-conf').value);
if(!species) return alert('Select a species first');
const btn = document.getElementById('compile-btn');
const status = document.getElementById('compile-status');
btn.disabled = true;
btn.innerText = 'Compiling...';
status.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-green-400', 'text-red-400');
status.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
status.innerText = 'Stitching audio files via FFmpeg...';
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({species, min_conf: conf, limit: 50})
});
const data = await res.json();

if(data.success) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';

}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 500;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 4000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
isAudioSetup = true;
}

const pageId = Object.keys(pages)[0];
if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;

}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];

labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#34d399',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

}

let audioInactivityTimer;
const audioPanel = document.getElementById('audio-panel-container');
const controlBar = document.getElementById('audio-control-bar');
const settingsPopup = document.getElementById('modal-settings-popup');

controlBar.classList.add('opacity-0');
}, 2500);
}
});
audioPanel.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
if(settingsPopup.classList.contains('hidden')) {
controlBar.classList.add('opacity-0');
}
clearTimeout(audioInactivityTimer);
});

e.stopPropagation();
settingsPopup.classList.toggle('hidden');
});

if (!settingsPopup.classList.contains('hidden') && !e.target.closest('#moda
settingsPopup.classList.add('hidden');

controlBar.classList.add('opacity-0');
}, 2500);
}
});
document.getElementById('sidebar-audio-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

audio.src = '/api/stream';
audio.play().then(() => {
btn.innerHTML = 'Disconnect';
btn.classList.add('bg-red-600', 'text-white');
dot.classList.replace('bg-slate-500', 'bg-[#4ade80]');
dot.classList.add('animate-pulse');

}).catch(e => {
dot.classList.replace('bg-slate-500', 'bg-yellow-500');
});
} else {
audio.pause();
audio.src = '';
btn.innerHTML = 'Connect Audio';
btn.classList.remove('bg-red-600', 'text-white');
dot.classList.replace('bg-[#4ade80]', 'bg-slate-500');
dot.classList.remove('animate-pulse');
}
});

async function pollLog() {
if(!document.getElementById('tab-log').classList.contains('active')) return
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/log');
const text = await res.text();
const out = document.getElementById('log-output');
if(out.innerText !== text) { out.innerText = text; out.scrollTop = out.
} catch(e) {}
}
init();

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}

return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}

function renderDiagnostics(data) {
const container = document.getElementById('health-stats');
container.innerHTML = '';
if(!data || data.length === 0) return;
let nightHits = 0, lowConf = 0;
data.forEach(d => {
if (d.Time) {
const hr = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0], 10);
if (!isNaN(hr) && (hr >= 22 || hr <= 4)) nightHits++;
}
if (d.Confidence < 0.5) lowConf++;
});
const nightRatio = ((nightHits / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
const confRatio = ((lowConf / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);

let nightColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let nightMsg = "Normal night
if (nightRatio > 15) { nightColor = 'text-yellow-400'; nightMsg = "Elevated

let confColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let confMsg = "Excellent conf
if (confRatio > 25) { confColor = 'text-yellow-400'; confMsg = "High volume

container.innerHTML = `
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
<span class="font-bold text-slate-300">Nighttime Triggers</span
<span class="font-mono font-bold ${nightColor}">${nightRatio}%<
</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${nightMsg}</p>
</div>
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">

<span class="font-bold text-slate-300">Sub-50% Confidence</span
<span class="font-mono font-bold ${confColor}">${confRatio}%</s
</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${confMsg}</p>
</div>
`;
}

function triggerCompile(species) {
document.getElementById('compile-species').value = species;
document.getElementById('compile-btn').click();
}
document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
const species = document.getElementById('compile-species').value;
const conf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('compile-conf').value);
if(!species) return alert('Select a species first');
const btn = document.getElementById('compile-btn');
const status = document.getElementById('compile-status');
btn.disabled = true;
btn.innerText = 'Compiling...';
status.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-green-400', 'text-red-400');
status.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
status.innerText = 'Stitching audio files via FFmpeg...';
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({species, min_conf: conf, limit: 50})
});
const data = await res.json();
if(data.success) {

status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';
}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 500;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 4000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
isAudioSetup = true;
}

async function fetchBirdImage(species) {
try {
const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&titles=${e
const res = await fetch(url);
const data = await res.json();

const pages = data.query.pages;
const pageId = Object.keys(pages)[0];
if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#34d399',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

}

} catch(e) {}

User prompt: Based on these screenshots, fix what is wrong (stock PHP shown for reference). If
there is some diagnostic step I can take to help figure out what's up let me know.
Response: My apologies for the formatting glitch. I previously used four backticks instead of the
standard three for the markdown blocks, which broke the Canvas rendering entirely and caused
the code to spill into the chat as raw text snippets.
I have reviewed both the original UI from your stock PHP screenshot and the broken layout from
the code snippet. Here is what has been addressed in this true Canvas-ready build:
�. The Canvas UI Fix: The markdown blocks have been meticulously formatted with exactly
three backticks and the precise html:index.html and python:birdnet_core.py labels to
ensure they load directly into your workspace.
2. Color Palette Restoration: I sampled the exact hex codes from your stock PHP screenshot.
The interface now uses the bright, saturated forest green (#2b6a43) for the background, with
deeply contrasting dark green panels (#1b452b) rather than the murky olive/gray tones.
3. Spectrogram Not Found Bug: The original BirdNET-Pi script sometimes names the
spectrogram simply as filename.png, but other times it leaves the audio extension intact,
creating filename.mp3.png. I have implemented a double-fallback system in both the
JavaScript image loader and the Python media router to check both file paths dynamically.
4. Modal Icons: The Wikipedia link is a stark "W", the AllAboutBirds link is now the requested
"i" (black text on a white circle), and the History Chart toggle is explicitly a black line with a
solid red fill under the curve.
5. Hidden Audio Compiler & Health Diagnostics: Because the previous generation failed to
render the Canvas, the frontend HTML for the Compiler and Diagnostics tabs was lost in the
text truncation. They are fully restored and active in this file.

def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'')
return config

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

let dbData = [];

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

const target = e.currentTarget;

document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
} catch (e) {
document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS

const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}

document.getElementById('sb-hour').innerText = hourData.length.toLocaleStri
document.getElementById('sb-species-today').innerText = spToday.toLocaleStr
document.getElementById('sb-species-total').innerText = totalSpecies.toLoca
const hourCounts = Array(24).fill(0);

if (!isNaN(hr)) hourCounts[hr]++;

async function renderAnalytics(data) {
if(!data || data.length === 0) return;
const ctx = document.getElementById('analytics-chart').getContext('2d');
if(activeChart) activeChart.destroy();

if (currentAnalyticsMode === 'matrix') {
const spCounts = {};
data.forEach(d => spCounts[d.Com_Name] = (spCounts[d.Com_Name] || 0) +
const topSp = Object.entries(spCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,2
const bData = []; let gMax = 0; const wMap = {};
data.forEach(d => {
if(topSp.includes(d.Com_Name) && d.Time) {
const h = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0],10);
const k = `${d.Com_Name}-${h}`;
wMap[k] = (wMap[k] || 0) + 1;
if(wMap[k] > gMax) gMax = wMap[k];
}
});

topSp.forEach((sp, yIdx) => {
for(let h=0; h<24; h++){
const v = wMap[`${sp}-${h}`];
if(v) bData.push({x:h, y:yIdx, r: Math.max(3, (v/gMax)*15), _v:

function renderDiagnostics(data) {

container.innerHTML = '';
if(!data || data.length === 0) return;

data.forEach(d => {
if (d.Time) {

if (!isNaN(hr) && (hr >= 22 || hr <= 4)) nightHits++;
}
if (d.Confidence < 0.5) lowConf++;
});
const nightRatio = ((nightHits / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);
const confRatio = ((lowConf / data.length) * 100).toFixed(1);

let nightColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let nightMsg = "Normal night
if (nightRatio > 15) { nightColor = 'text-yellow-400'; nightMsg = "Elevated

let confColor = 'text-[var(--bn-highlight)]'; let confMsg = "Excellent conf
if (confRatio > 25) { confColor = 'text-yellow-400'; confMsg = "High volume

container.innerHTML = `
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
<span class="font-bold text-slate-300">Nighttime Triggers</span

</div>
<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">${nightMsg}</p>
</div>
<div class="bg-[var(--bn-bg)] p-4 rounded border border-[var(--bn-borde
<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">

<span class="font-mono font-bold ${confColor}">${confRatio}%</s

}

const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 500;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 4000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
isAudioSetup = true;
}

} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);

curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

User prompt: Lots of improvements this time! From a design standpoint, can we switch this to
being vertical panels rather than horizontal? It fits the horizonticality of the spectrogram and what's
typical of a title area (rather than what looks like a sidebar), allows more to fit on the screen. Still
lacking high pass and low pass settings, these simple toggles are too restrictive to be very useful.
More variety like in the screenshot I sent earlier. For the graphs, like the one in the 2nd attached
screenshot, background would be better white (prevent clash and for better clarity), and colors
should have more intent. Example: rain not being blue (intuitive). Sub 50% confidence thing is
useless as my confidence threshold is set to 70%. Nighttime thing not very useful, just look at it.
Playhead does not move smoothly through playback window, should move at constant speed but it
instead ticks forward every ~second. Work on all that and whatever else you deem a good idea for
now

def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'')
return config

list_fd, list_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix='.txt', text=True)
with open(list_path, 'w') as f:
for vf in valid_files:
f.write(f"file '{vf}'\n")

out_filename = f"{target_species.replace(' ', '_')}_Mix_{int(time.time(
out_filepath = os.path.join(mix_dir, out_filename)

cmd = ['ffmpeg', '-y', '-f', 'concat', '-safe', '0', '-i', list_path, '
subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, che
os.remove(list_path)
web_path = out_filepath.replace(base_dir, '')
if web_path.startswith('/'): web_path = web_path[1:]

self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/json')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"success": True, "file": web_path, "count"
except Exception as e:
self.send_error(500, str(e))
def do_GET(self):
if self.path == '/api/detections':
db_path = self.get_db_path()
if not db_path:
self.send_error(404, "Database not found")
return
try:
conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("SELECT Date, Time, Sci_Name, Com_Name, Confidence FROM
rows = cursor.fetchall()
col_names = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
output = io.StringIO()
writer = csv.writer(output)
writer.writerow(col_names)
writer.writerows(rows)
csv_data = output.getvalue()
conn.close()
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'text/csv')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(csv_data.encode('utf-8'))
except Exception as e:

self.send_error(500, str(e))

elif self.path == '/api/system':
try:
temp = subprocess.getoutput("cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp"
temp_c = round(int(temp) / 1000.0, 1) if temp.isdigit() else 0.0
mem = subprocess.getoutput("free -m | awk 'NR==2 {printf \"%.1f\", $3*1
disk = subprocess.getoutput("df -h / | awk 'NR==2 {print $5}'").replace
uptime = subprocess.getoutput("uptime -p").replace('up ', '')
data = {"temp": temp_c, "memory": float(mem) if mem else 0, "disk": int
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/json')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(json.dumps(data).encode())
except Exception as e:
self.send_error(500, str(e))
elif self.path == '/api/config':
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/json')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(json.dumps(get_config()).encode())

elif self.path == '/api/log':
try:
log_output = subprocess.getoutput("journalctl -u birdnet_analysis.servi
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'text/plain')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(log_output.encode())
except Exception as e:
self.send_error(500, str(e))
elif self.path == '/api/gallery':
try:
recent = []
best_map = {}
valid_exts = {'.wav', '.mp3', '.flac', '.m4a'}
base_dir = os.path.expanduser('~/BirdSongs')

for root, _, files in os.walk(base_dir):
if "streamdata" in root.lower() or "mixes" in root.lower() or root
for file in files:
if os.path.splitext(file)[1].lower() in valid_exts and "birdnet
filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
try:
stat = os.stat(filepath)
match = re.search(r"^(.*?)-(\d{2,3})-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}"
species = match.group(1).replace("_", " ") if match els

conf = float(match.group(2)) / 100.0 if match else 0.5
web_path = filepath.replace(base_dir, '')
if web_path.startswith('/'): web_path = web_path[1:]

file_obj = {
"filepath": web_path,
"filename": file,
"species": species,
"confidence": conf,
"size_kb": stat.st_size // 1024,
"mtime": stat.st_mtime,
"date_str": re.search(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", file).g
}

recent.append(file_obj)
if species not in best_map or conf > best_map[species][
best_map[species] = file_obj
except Exception: pass
recent.sort(key=lambda x: x["mtime"], reverse=True)
payload = {"recent": recent[:200], "best": list(best_map.values())}
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/json')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(json.dumps(payload).encode())
except Exception as e:
self.send_error(500, str(e))
elif self.path == '/api/stream':
try:
config = get_config()
password = config.get('BIRDNETPI_PASSWORD', '')
host = self.headers.get('Host', '127.0.0.1').split(':')[0]

stream_url = f"http://birdnet:{password}@{host}:8000/stream" if passwor
req = urllib.request.Request(stream_url)
with urllib.request.urlopen(req) as response:
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-Type', 'audio/mpeg')
self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')
self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
self.end_headers()
while True:
chunk = response.read(8192)
if not chunk: break
self.wfile.write(chunk)
except Exception as e:

self.send_error(503, str(e))
else:
if self.path == '/':
self.path = '/index.html'

if self.path.startswith('/images/'):
clean_path = urllib.parse.unquote(self.path.replace('/images/', ''))
target_path = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~/BirdNET-Pi/homepage/im
if os.path.exists(target_path):
try:
with open(target_path, 'rb') as f:
self.send_response(200)
if target_path.endswith('.svg'):
self.send_header('Content-type', 'image/svg+xml')
elif target_path.endswith('.png'):
self.send_header('Content-type', 'image/png')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(f.read())
return
except Exception: pass

if any(self.path.endswith(ext) for ext in ['.mp3', '.wav', '.png', '.jpg',
clean_path = urllib.parse.unquote(self.path.lstrip('/'))
target_path = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~/BirdSongs'), clean_pat
if not os.path.exists(target_path) and target_path.endswith('.png'):
alt_mp3 = target_path[:-4] + '.mp3.png'
alt_wav = target_path[:-4] + '.wav.png'
if os.path.exists(alt_mp3):
target_path = alt_mp3
elif os.path.exists(alt_wav):
target_path = alt_wav

if os.path.exists(target_path):
try:
with open(target_path, 'rb') as f:
self.send_response(200)
if target_path.endswith('.png'): self.send_header('Contentelif target_path.endswith('.jpg') or target_path.endswith('
else: self.send_header('Content-type', 'audio/mpeg')
self.end_headers()
self.wfile.write(f.read())
return
except Exception: pass
try:
super().do_GET()
except ConnectionError:

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
} catch (e) {
document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}

function triggerCompile(species) {

document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
const species = document.getElementById('compile-species').value;
const conf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('compile-conf').value);
if(!species) return alert('Select a species first');
const btn = document.getElementById('compile-btn');
const status = document.getElementById('compile-status');
btn.disabled = true;
btn.innerText = 'Compiling...';
status.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-green-400', 'text-red-400');
status.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
status.innerText = 'Stitching audio files via FFmpeg...';
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},

body: JSON.stringify({species, min_conf: conf, limit: 50})
});
const data = await res.json();

if(data.success) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';
}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
isAudioSetup = true;
}

try {
const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&titles=${e

const res = await fetch(url);
const data = await res.json();
const pages = data.query.pages;
const pageId = Object.keys(pages)[0];
if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

const imgPath = cleanUrl.substring(0, cleanUrl.lastIndexOf('.')) + '.png';
const specImg = document.getElementById('modal-spectro-img');
const specErr = document.getElementById('modal-spectro-error');
specImg.dataset.retried = 'false';
specImg.src = imgPath;
specImg.onload = () => {
specImg.classList.remove('hidden');
specErr.classList.add('hidden');
};
specImg.onerror = () => {
if (specImg.dataset.retried === 'false') {
specImg.dataset.retried = 'true';
specImg.src = cleanUrl + '.png';
} else {
specImg.classList.add('hidden');
specErr.classList.remove('hidden');
}
};

document.getElementById('history-popover').classList.add('hidden');
drawModalHistoryChart(species, document.getElementById('history-timeframe')

document.getElementById('modal-progress').style.width = '0%';
document.getElementById('modal-playhead').style.left = '0%';
document.getElementById('modal-time').innerText = '0:00';

}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();

let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]

},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

User prompt: Nice! Seems like you addressed everything I mentioned in that last chat. Still sent as
code snippets but they worked, so I won't complain. Live audio stream (and consequently the live
spectrogram we had a long time ago but scrapped (it's also featured on the default site)) still not
working, also for the audio compile it seems we've moved back to simple splicing together.
Admittedly this is faster, but there used to be some processing going on (do you have access to the
details of this in the pdf? i forgot if it was in there or not) which made it a whole lot nicer
Response: I'm glad the snippets worked out, even if the Canvas UI was being stubborn. I'll stick to
standard markdown code blocks here so you can easily copy them.
I checked the handover document (PDF) regarding the audio compiler. It actually explicitly states
that the system uses ffmpeg to stitch the MP3s together without re-encoding in order to save CPU.
It doesn't detail any advanced audio processing. However, if the old mix sounded smoother, it's
highly likely it used a padding or silence filter between the jump-cuts.
To bring that professional, digestible feeling back to the compiled audio without blowing up your
Pi's CPU with heavy normalization filters, I've updated the Python compiler script to generate a 1second silence pad on the fly and seamlessly intersperse it between the detections during the
compile phase.
For the live stream, proxying an infinite Icecast stream through Python's HTTP server can
occasionally get tangled if the hostname resolution trips up. I've hardcoded the proxy stream URL
to target 127.0.0.1 locally, which ensures it reliably intercepts the Icecast daemon. I've also
hooked the sidebar <audio> element up to the Web Audio API to bring back the Live Spectrogram,
anchored directly above the stream controls in the sidebar.
Here are the updated files:

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

let dbData = [];

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}

}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

const target = e.currentTarget;
target.classList.add('active');
const tabId = target.getAttribute('data-tab');
document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);
applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
} catch (e) {

document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}
let dashChart = null;
function renderDashboard(data) {

const currHour = new Date().getHours();

const hourData = todayData.filter(d => d.Time && parseInt(d.Time.split(':')

const spToday = new Set(todayData.map(d => d.Com_Name)).size;

document.getElementById('dash-today').innerText = todayData.length.toLocale
document.getElementById('dash-hour').innerText = hourData.length.toLocaleSt
document.getElementById('dash-species-total').innerText = totalSpecies.toLo

document.getElementById('sb-total').innerText = dbData.length.toLocaleStrin
document.getElementById('sb-today').innerText = todayData.length.toLocaleSt
document.getElementById('sb-hour').innerText = hourData.length.toLocaleStri
document.getElementById('sb-species-today').innerText = spToday.toLocaleStr
document.getElementById('sb-species-total').innerText = totalSpecies.toLoca
const hourCounts = Array(24).fill(0);
data.forEach(d => {

if (d.Time) {
const hr = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0], 10);
if (!isNaN(hr)) hourCounts[hr]++;
}
});

const ctx = document.getElementById('dash-hourly-chart').getContext('2d');

},

});
}

async function renderAnalytics(data) {
if(!data || data.length === 0) return;
const ctx = document.getElementById('analytics-chart').getContext('2d');
if(activeChart) activeChart.destroy();

const commonScales = {
y: { grid: { color: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.05)' }, ticks: { color: '#475569' } }
x: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { color: '#475569' } }
};

if (currentAnalyticsMode === 'matrix') {
const spCounts = {};
data.forEach(d => spCounts[d.Com_Name] = (spCounts[d.Com_Name] || 0) +
const topSp = Object.entries(spCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,2
const bData = []; let gMax = 0; const wMap = {};
data.forEach(d => {
if(topSp.includes(d.Com_Name) && d.Time) {
const h = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0],10);

const k = `${d.Com_Name}-${h}`;
wMap[k] = (wMap[k] || 0) + 1;
if(wMap[k] > gMax) gMax = wMap[k];
}
});

const hourCounts = Array(24).fill(0);
data.forEach(d => {
if (d.Time) {
const hr = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0], 10);
if (!isNaN(hr)) hourCounts[hr]++;
}
});
const peakHour = hourCounts.indexOf(Math.max(...hourCounts));
const ampm = peakHour >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
const displayHour = peakHour % 12 || 12;

container.innerHTML = `

<div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">

</div>

document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {

const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({species, min_conf: conf, limit: 50})
});
const data = await res.json();

if(data.success) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';
}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';

lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
isAudioSetup = true;
}
let liveAudioCtx, liveAnalyser, liveSource, liveDataArray;
let isLiveAudioSetup = false;
let liveAnimId;
const liveCanvas = document.getElementById('live-spectro-canvas');
const liveCtx = liveCanvas.getContext('2d');
function initLiveSpectrogram() {
if (isLiveAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
liveAudioCtx = new AudioContext();
liveAnalyser = liveAudioCtx.createAnalyser();
liveAnalyser.fftSize = 512;
const sbAudio = document.getElementById('sidebar-audio');
liveSource = liveAudioCtx.createMediaElementSource(sbAudio);
liveSource.connect(liveAnalyser);
liveAnalyser.connect(liveAudioCtx.destination);
liveDataArray = new Uint8Array(liveAnalyser.frequencyBinCount);
isLiveAudioSetup = true;
liveCanvas.width = liveCanvas.offsetWidth;
liveCanvas.height = liveCanvas.offsetHeight;
}
function drawLiveSpectrogram() {
if (!document.getElementById('sidebar-audio').paused) {
liveAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(drawLiveSpectrogram);
liveAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(liveDataArray);
const w = liveCanvas.width;
const h = liveCanvas.height;
const imgData = liveCtx.getImageData(1, 0, w - 1, h);
liveCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
const validBins = Math.floor(liveDataArray.length * 0.75);
const binHeight = h / validBins;

for (let i = 0; i < validBins; i++) {
const val = liveDataArray[i];
const r = val > 128 ? 255 : val * 2;
const g = val > 192 ? 255 : (val > 128 ? (val - 128) * 2 : 0);
const b = val > 200 ? 255 : (val < 128 ? val : 0);
liveCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
liveCtx.fillRect(w - 1, h - (i * binHeight), 1, binHeight);
}
} else {
cancelAnimationFrame(liveAnimId);
}
}

if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);

}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

async function pollLog() {

const res = await fetch('/api/log');
const text = await res.text();
const out = document.getElementById('log-output');
if(out.innerText !== text) { out.innerText = text; out.scrollTop = out.
} catch(e) {}
}
init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

User prompt: We didn't use padding, it was crossfading if I remember right. I'd also like a 1d setting
on the audio compiler, and possibly a day/timeframe selector (like that calendar view thing). Also
you should be able to click on the spectrogram when playing back media to pause like you can a
youtube video. Also any other suggestions? What's next
Response: I can certainly implement those updates. Returning to the acrossfade FFmpeg filter
will reintroduce that smooth, processed sound to your compilations.
Based on the [Errno 111] Connection refused error in your terminal screenshot, the Icecast
daemon is rejecting requests sent explicitly to 127.0.0.1. I have rewritten the stream proxy to
dynamically poll localhost, 127.0.0.1, and your raw host IP to guarantee it finds the correct
binding.

Implemented Updates
• Audio Compiler Crossfading: I have removed the silence pad and injected a dynamic
FFmpeg -filter_complex string that applies a 1.5-second triangular crossfade
(acrossfade=d=1.5:c1=tri:c2=tri) across all matched audio files.
• Timeframe Expansions: The compiler now features a 24-Hour option, as well as a Custom
Date Range selector that uses native HTML5 calendar inputs. Crucially, the Python backend
now strictly obeys these dates when scanning the filesystem for the mix.
• Spectrogram Play/Pause: The modal spectrogram image is now a functional, clickable area
that toggles media playback.
• Stream Connectivity: Built a fallback loop in the Python proxy to resolve the Errno 111
connection crash.

Future Directions to Consider

• Dynamic Data Visualizations: Implementing a "Dawn Chorus" heat map to visualize
morning vs. evening density by species.
• Live WebSockets: Upgrading from standard polling to WebSockets so new detections
appear on your dashboard the exact millisecond they happen.
• Mobile-Optimized Drawer: Refactoring the floating audio controls into a bottom-sheet
drawer for better usability on mobile devices.
Here are the updated files:

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}

loader.classList.remove('hidden');

updateSystemStats();

} finally {

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
if (daysStr === 'custom') {
const start = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
const end = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
if(!start || !end) return [...sourceData];
return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date >= start && d.Date <= end);
}
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}

const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
isAudioSetup = true;
}
let liveAudioCtx, liveAnalyser, liveSource, liveDataArray;
let isLiveAudioSetup = false;
let liveAnimId;
const liveCanvas = document.getElementById('live-spectro-canvas');
const liveCtx = liveCanvas.getContext('2d');
function initLiveSpectrogram() {
if (isLiveAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
liveAudioCtx = new AudioContext();
liveAnalyser = liveAudioCtx.createAnalyser();
liveAnalyser.fftSize = 512;
const sbAudio = document.getElementById('sidebar-audio');
liveSource = liveAudioCtx.createMediaElementSource(sbAudio);
liveSource.connect(liveAnalyser);
liveAnalyser.connect(liveAudioCtx.destination);
liveDataArray = new Uint8Array(liveAnalyser.frequencyBinCount);

isLiveAudioSetup = true;
liveCanvas.width = liveCanvas.offsetWidth;
liveCanvas.height = liveCanvas.offsetHeight;
}
function drawLiveSpectrogram() {
if (!document.getElementById('sidebar-audio').paused) {
liveAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(drawLiveSpectrogram);
liveAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(liveDataArray);
const w = liveCanvas.width;
const h = liveCanvas.height;
const imgData = liveCtx.getImageData(1, 0, w - 1, h);
liveCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
const validBins = Math.floor(liveDataArray.length * 0.75);
const binHeight = h / validBins;
for (let i = 0; i < validBins; i++) {
const val = liveDataArray[i];
const r = val > 128 ? 255 : val * 2;
const g = val > 192 ? 255 : (val > 128 ? (val - 128) * 2 : 0);
const b = val > 200 ? 255 : (val < 128 ? val : 0);
liveCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
liveCtx.fillRect(w - 1, h - (i * binHeight), 1, binHeight);
}
} else {
cancelAnimationFrame(liveAnimId);
}
}

} catch (e) {}

}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);

}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}
