Are there any specific mobile layout adjustments you'd like to prioritize?

User prompt: How can we troubleshoot this audio stream thing, it's been going on for quite some
time. Wonderful job on the media playback, took a bit but we got there and it's great at the moment.

I'm not worried about mobile layout adjustments quite yet, I'd rather build it out to be more fully
featured and then work out optimizations for desktop and mobile view. What features should we
work on next do you think
Response: I'm glad the media playback is feeling dialed in!
For the audio stream, [Errno 111] Connection refused means the Python server is knocking
on port 8000, but absolutely nothing is answering. This usually means one of three things: Icecast
is down, it's running on a different port, or it's explicitly configured to ignore localhost and only
listen to the local network IP.
Here are the diagnostic steps to run in your Pi's terminal to figure out exactly where the disconnect
is:
1. Check if Icecast is actually running and healthy:
sudo systemctl status icecast2
2. Check which port Icecast is bound to:
sudo netstat -tulpn | grep LISTEN | grep 8000
(If this returns nothing, Icecast isn't using 8000. We can check your config to see what it is using).
3. Check your BirdNET-Pi config for custom ports:
cat ~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf | grep PORT
4. Test the direct connection locally:
curl -I http://localhost:8000/stream
If the stock PHP site is currently playing the live audio just fine, you can also inspect that page
(Right Click -> Inspect -> Network tab) and see exactly what URL the native audio player is
fetching. If we know that exact URL, we can either hardcode it into the Python proxy or bypass the
proxy entirely and let the HTML reach out to it directly.
As for what to build out next, here are a few feature directions that add significant utility without
worrying about layout optimizations just yet:
• Advanced Database Explorer: Right now, we just feed the most recent 30 detections to the
dashboard. We could build a dedicated "Database" tab with a powerful search interface to
query your entire SQLite history by species, date range, time of day, and confidence, with a
button to export those specific queries to CSV.
• WebSockets for True Real-Time UI: Instead of the frontend polling the Python backend
every 3 seconds for the log and stats, we could upgrade the Python server to use
WebSockets. This means the moment a bird is detected and saved to the database, the
server pushes it to the UI instantly, making the dashboard completely live.
• "Highlight Reel" Auto-Compiler: Expanding the Audio Compiler to have a "Generate
Weekly Highlights" button. It would automatically comb the last 7 days, find the highest-

confidence recording for every unique species, and stitch them into a single track with the
crossfade effect.
• Spectrogram Customization: Adding a settings gear specifically for the live spectrogram
and the static spectrograms that lets you swap out the color maps (e.g., Viridis, Inferno,
Magma) or adjust the contrast/brightness thresholds via the HTML5 canvas to make faint
calls easier to read.

User prompt: Here are the results
Response: That screenshot is incredibly helpful. It tells us exactly why the Python proxy is throwing
the [Errno 111] Connection refused error, and it comes down to a mix of a typo and a
"zombie" service.

1. The Typo Hiding the Port
In your third command, you typed cat ~/BirdNET-PI/birdnet.conf | grep PORT. Linux is
case-sensitive, so the capital I in PI caused the "No such file or directory" error.
Run this corrected command to see if Icecast was moved off the default port 8000 during a
previous configuration:
cat ~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf | grep -i port

2. The "Zombie" Icecast Service
Your first command shows icecast2.service as active (exited). In systemd, an actual
background daemon should say active (running). The (exited) status means the startup
script ran without throwing a fatal error code, but the Icecast process itself either immediately
crashed or failed to background properly. This completely aligns with your netstat and curl
results showing absolutely nothing listening on port 8000.
Here are the exact diagnostic commands to find out why Icecast is dying:
Check the dedicated Icecast error log:
cat /var/log/icecast2/error.log | tail -n 20
Check the system journal for the exact crash reason:
sudo journalctl -u icecast2.service -n 50 --no-pager
Force a restart and check if it holds:
sudo systemctl restart icecast2 && sleep 2 && sudo systemctl status icecast2

Future Feature Roadmap
Once we get the Icecast daemon healthy and the live spectrogram humming, here are a few strong

directions for the next build phase:
• Advanced Database Explorer: Currently, we are just feeding the most recent 30 detections
to the dashboard feed. We could build a dedicated "Database" tab with a powerful search
interface to query your entire SQLite history by species, date range, time of day, and
confidence, complete with a CSV export function.
• True Real-Time UI via WebSockets: Instead of the frontend polling the Python backend
every few seconds for the log and stats, we could upgrade the Python server to use
WebSockets. This means the exact millisecond a bird is detected and saved to the database,
the server pushes it to the UI instantly.
• "Highlight Reel" Auto-Compiler: Expanding the new Audio Compiler to have a "Generate
Weekly Highlights" button. It would automatically comb the last 7 days, find the absolute
highest-confidence recording for every unique species, and stitch them into a single track.
• Spectrogram Customization: Adding a specific settings gear for the spectrograms that lets
you swap out the color maps (e.g., Viridis, Inferno, Magma) or adjust the contrast/brightness
thresholds dynamically via the HTML5 canvas to make faint calls easier to read.

Response: That screenshot is exactly what we wanted to see! The restart command successfully
revived the daemon.
If you look at the middle of the terminal output, icecast2.service has changed from active
(exited) to active (running), and it shows the active process ID (1034422). Those WARN
CONFIG messages at the very bottom are just standard XML parsing warnings from Icecast's
default configuration file and are completely harmless.
Since the daemon is officially breathing again, your Python sidecar server will finally be able to
proxy the stream.
Refresh your dashboard, hit that Connect Audio button in the sidebar, and you should
immediately hear the live feed and see the live spectrogram canvas light up.
Assuming the stream is now humming along perfectly, we have a solid, fully functional foundation.
Which of those next-level features would you like to build into the UI next—the Database Explorer,
the real-time WebSockets, the Highlight Reel generator, or the Spectrogram Customizer?

User prompt: Stream working. Database explorer sounds excellent
Response: The Database Explorer is a perfect addition for turning this dashboard into a true
analysis tool. Because our Headless Hybrid architecture already securely pulls your entire SQLite
database into memory when the dashboard loads, we don't even need to modify the Python
backend or hit the server to run queries. We can build a lightning-fast search engine entirely in the
frontend.
I have added the Database tab to your navigation. It features:

• Granular Search: Filter your entire history by Species, Date Range, Time of Day, and a
minimum Confidence threshold.
• Live Results Table: The table instantly updates as you adjust filters, providing the exact
timestamp, confidence score, and a quick-play button to pull that specific audio file into the
spectrogram modal. To prevent browser lag if your database grows to hundreds of thousands
of rows, the UI safely caps the visual display at 500 rows.
• CSV Export: A dedicated "Export CSV" button that bypasses the display limit. It takes
whatever exact filters you've applied and downloads the full matching dataset directly to your
local machine as a .csv file.

CONFIG_PATH = os.path.expanduser('~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf')
def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'')
return config
class SidecarHandler(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):

def do_GET(self):
if self.path == '/api/detections':
db_path = self.get_db_path()
if not db_path:
self.send_error(404, "Database not found")
return
try:

cursor = conn.cursor()
cursor.execute("SELECT Date, Time, Sci_Name, Com_Name, Confidence FROM
rows = cursor.fetchall()
col_names = [d[0] for d in cursor.description]
output = io.StringIO()
writer = csv.writer(output)

writer.writerow(col_names)
writer.writerows(rows)
csv_data = output.getvalue()
conn.close()
self.send_response(200)
self.send_header('Content-type', 'text/csv')
self.end_headers()

except Exception as e:

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

});
});

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}

} finally {

}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
if (daysStr === 'custom') {
const start = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
const end = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
if(!start || !end) return [...sourceData];
return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date >= start && d.Date <= end);
}
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}

renderDashboard(filtered);

}

function filterDatabase() {
const sp = document.getElementById('db-filter-species').value;
const dStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-start').value;
const dEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-end').value;
const tStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-start').value;
const tEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-end').value;
const minConf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('db-filter-conf').value)
let res = dbData;
if(sp !== 'all') res = res.filter(d => d.Com_Name === sp);
if(dStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Date >= dStart);
if(dEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Date <= dEnd);
if(tStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Time >= tStart);
if(tEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Time <= tEnd);
if(minConf > 0) res = res.filter(d => d.Confidence >= minConf);
renderDatabaseTable(res);
window.currentDbExport = res;
}
function renderDatabaseTable(data) {

return;
}

tbody.innerHTML = displayData.map(d => {
const pct = (d.Confidence * 100).toFixed(0);
return `<tr class="hover:bg-[var(--bn-bg)] transition-colors border-b b
<td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${d.Date}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${d.Time}</td>
<td class="p-3 font-bold text-white">${d.Com_Name}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-slate-400 italic">${d.Sci_Name}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-[var(--bn-highlight)] font-mono">${pct}%</td>
<td class="p-3 text-right">
<button onclick="searchAndPlay('${d.Com_Name}', '${d.Sci_Name}'
<svg class="w-6 h-6 inline" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0
</button>
</td>
</tr>`;
}).join('');
}

function exportDatabaseCSV() {
const data = window.currentDbExport || dbData;
if(data.length === 0) return alert("No data to export.");
const headers = ["Date", "Time", "Sci_Name", "Com_Name", "Confidence"];
const csvRows = [headers.join(',')];

data.forEach(d => {
csvRows.push(`${d.Date},${d.Time},"${d.Sci_Name}","${d.Com_Name}",${d.C
});
const blob = new Blob([csvRows.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv' });
const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
const a = document.createElement('a');
a.setAttribute('href', url);
a.setAttribute('download', `BirdNET_Export_${getTodayStr()}.csv`);
a.click();
}

const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
isAudioSetup = true;
}
let liveAudioCtx, liveAnalyser, liveSource, liveDataArray;
let isLiveAudioSetup = false;
let liveAnimId;
const liveCanvas = document.getElementById('live-spectro-canvas');
const liveCtx = liveCanvas.getContext('2d');
function initLiveSpectrogram() {
if (isLiveAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
liveAudioCtx = new AudioContext();
liveAnalyser = liveAudioCtx.createAnalyser();
liveAnalyser.fftSize = 512;
const sbAudio = document.getElementById('sidebar-audio');

liveSource = liveAudioCtx.createMediaElementSource(sbAudio);
liveSource.connect(liveAnalyser);
liveAnalyser.connect(liveAudioCtx.destination);
liveDataArray = new Uint8Array(liveAnalyser.frequencyBinCount);
isLiveAudioSetup = true;
liveCanvas.width = liveCanvas.offsetWidth;
liveCanvas.height = liveCanvas.offsetHeight;
}
function drawLiveSpectrogram() {
if (!document.getElementById('sidebar-audio').paused) {
liveAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(drawLiveSpectrogram);
liveAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(liveDataArray);
const w = liveCanvas.width;
const h = liveCanvas.height;
const imgData = liveCtx.getImageData(1, 0, w - 1, h);
liveCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
const validBins = Math.floor(liveDataArray.length * 0.75);
const binHeight = h / validBins;
for (let i = 0; i < validBins; i++) {
const val = liveDataArray[i];
const r = val > 128 ? 255 : val * 2;
const g = val > 192 ? 255 : (val > 128 ? (val - 128) * 2 : 0);
const b = val > 200 ? 255 : (val < 128 ? val : 0);
liveCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
liveCtx.fillRect(w - 1, h - (i * binHeight), 1, binHeight);
}
} else {
cancelAnimationFrame(liveAnimId);
}
}

} catch (e) {}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {

const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

User prompt: Nice, what do you suggest next? Think big. This will replace the stock PHP.

Response: To completely replace the legacy PHP architecture, we have to bridge the gap between
a passive data viewer and an active system administrator. If we are severing the cord entirely and
aiming for a modern, standalone application, here are the major pillars to build out next.

The Configuration & Control Center (The True PHP Killer)
To sever the cord from the legacy system, the Python sidecar must inherit full administrative
control. We need a secure System Configuration module that maps directly to birdnet.conf.
This allows the manipulation of latitude/longitude, global confidence thresholds, USB audio
interface selection, and system passwords directly from the browser. A dedicated backend
endpoint would safely write these changes and execute system-level service restarts without ever
requiring terminal access.

WebSockets & Real-Time Telemetry
Standard API polling works, but it isn't modern. Upgrading the Python backend to support
WebSockets changes the entire paradigm. The exact millisecond the underlying BirdNET analysis
service registers a detection, the server pushes that event to the frontend. The dashboard updates
instantaneously, the live log streams natively without requesting data, and we could implement
real-time browser push notifications for user-defined "rare bird" alerts.

The Cloud Integration Hub
Raspberry Pi SD cards are notoriously fragile, and users accumulate massive, scientifically
valuable datasets. We can introduce an integration layer for automated, scheduled SQLite
database and audio mix backups to external cloud storage. Furthermore, we can build a formatting
tool to easily batch-export these specific local detections directly into eBird or other citizen science
databases.

Macro-Trend Visualizations
If this application is going to house years of ecological data, it needs visualizations capable of
handling macro-trends.
• Annual Heatmaps: A GitHub-style calendar heatmap displaying the densest days of
acoustic activity over a 365-day period for quick visual scanning.
• Migration Mapping: Line chart overlays comparing the arrival dates of specific migratory
species across multiple years, utilizing the existing Open-Meteo weather integration to track
environmental shifts and behavioral patterns.

User prompt: Sure, I agree. Do these help?
Response: That screenshot is the exact blueprint for our next major strike. The native PHP
interface uses that horizontal submenu to handle all the deep system administration, service
management, and configuration routing.

If we are going to sever the cord from PHP entirely, we have to absorb all of those administrative
tools into our Python/JS architecture.
We are going to build the System & Configuration Hub. I have completely gutted the old
placeholder "System Tools" tab and transformed it into a full-scale administration panel.

The Upgrades in this Build:
• Two-Way Configuration (Read/Write): The Python backend now supports POST requests to
/api/config. The UI dynamically builds a form based on your birdnet.conf file, allows you
to change settings like Latitude, Longitude, and Confidence thresholds directly in the browser,
and safely saves them back to the Pi.
• Service Controller: I've added a "Services" module that polls the actual systemd status of
birdnet_analysis.service and icecast2.service in real-time. It includes interactive
buttons to restart these core services directly from the UI, bypassing the need for an SSH
terminal.
• Species List Management Structure: The UI now features the framework for editing your
Custom, Included, and Excluded species lists natively.
Here are the upgraded files:

CONFIG_PATH = os.path.expanduser('~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf')
def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'\n')
return config

def update_config(updates):
if not os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH): return False
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
lines = f.readlines()
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'w') as f:
for line in lines:
updated = False
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key = line.split('=', 1)[0].strip()
if key in updates:
f.write(f'{key}="{updates[key]}"\n')
updated = True
if not updated:
f.write(line)
return True

class ThreadingServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
daemon_threads = True
allow_reuse_address = True
if __name__ == '__main__':
with ThreadingServer(("", PORT), SidecarHandler) as httpd:
print(f"BirdNET Core Server active at http://localhost:{PORT}")
try: httpd.serve_forever()
except KeyboardInterrupt: httpd.server_close()

function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({
Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}

return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(el => {
el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(nav => nav.classList.rem
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.

const target = e.currentTarget;
target.classList.add('active');
const tabId = target.getAttribute('data-tab');
document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
if (tabId === 'tools') populateConfigForm();
});
});

}

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
window.currentDbExport = dbData;

const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
populateDatabaseFilter();
filterDatabase();
} catch (e) {
document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
if (daysStr === 'custom') {
const start = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
const end = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
if(!start || !end) return [...sourceData];
return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date >= start && d.Date <= end);
}
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}
let dashChart = null;
function renderDashboard(data) {
const todayStr = getTodayStr();

const currHour = new Date().getHours();

const todayData = dbData.filter(d => d.Date === todayStr);
const hourData = todayData.filter(d => d.Time && parseInt(d.Time.split(':')

const spToday = new Set(todayData.map(d => d.Com_Name)).size;

document.getElementById('dash-total').innerText = dbData.length.toLocaleStr
document.getElementById('dash-today').innerText = todayData.length.toLocale
document.getElementById('dash-hour').innerText = hourData.length.toLocaleSt
document.getElementById('dash-species-total').innerText = totalSpecies.toLo

document.getElementById('sb-total').innerText = dbData.length.toLocaleStrin
document.getElementById('sb-today').innerText = todayData.length.toLocaleSt
document.getElementById('sb-hour').innerText = hourData.length.toLocaleStri
document.getElementById('sb-species-today').innerText = spToday.toLocaleStr
document.getElementById('sb-species-total').innerText = totalSpecies.toLoca
const hourCounts = Array(24).fill(0);
data.forEach(d => {
if (d.Time) {
const hr = parseInt(d.Time.split(':')[0], 10);
if (!isNaN(hr)) hourCounts[hr]++;
}
});

const ctx = document.getElementById('dash-hourly-chart').getContext('2d');
if (dashChart) dashChart.destroy();
dashChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'bar',

},
options: {
responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: {
scales: { y: { grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)' }, ticks: {
}
});

const feed = document.getElementById('dash-feed');
feed.innerHTML = '';
data.slice(0, 30).forEach(d => {
const pct = (parseFloat(d.Confidence)*100).toFixed(0);
feed.innerHTML += `
<div onclick="searchAndPlay('${d.Com_Name}', '${d.Sci_Name}', '${d.
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">

<div>

<span class="text-xs text-slate-300">${d.Date} ${d.Time
</div>
</div>
<span class="text-xs font-mono font-bold ${pct < 70 ? 'text-yel
</div>
`;
});
}

function populateDatabaseFilter() {

function filterDatabase() {
const sp = document.getElementById('db-filter-species').value;
const dStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-start').value;
const dEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-end').value;
const tStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-start').value;
const tEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-end').value;
const minConf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('db-filter-conf').value)
let res = dbData;
if(sp !== 'all') res = res.filter(d => d.Com_Name === sp);
if(dStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Date >= dStart);
if(dEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Date <= dEnd);
if(tStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Time >= tStart);
if(tEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Time <= tEnd);
if(minConf > 0) res = res.filter(d => d.Confidence >= minConf);
renderDatabaseTable(res);
window.currentDbExport = res;
}
function renderDatabaseTable(data) {
const tbody = document.getElementById('db-table-body');
const limit = 500;
const displayData = data.slice(0, limit);

document.getElementById('db-results-count').innerText = `${data.length.toLo

if(displayData.length === 0) {
tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-4 text-center text-slat
return;
}

tbody.innerHTML = displayData.map(d => {
const pct = (d.Confidence * 100).toFixed(0);
return `<tr class="hover:bg-[var(--bn-bg)] transition-colors border-b b
<td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${d.Date}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-slate-300">${d.Time}</td>
<td class="p-3 font-bold text-white">${d.Com_Name}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-slate-400 italic">${d.Sci_Name}</td>
<td class="p-3 text-[var(--bn-highlight)] font-mono">${pct}%</td>
<td class="p-3 text-right">
<button onclick="searchAndPlay('${d.Com_Name}', '${d.Sci_Name}'
<svg class="w-6 h-6 inline" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0
</button>
</td>
</tr>`;
}).join('');
}
function exportDatabaseCSV() {
const data = window.currentDbExport || dbData;
if(data.length === 0) return alert("No data to export.");
const headers = ["Date", "Time", "Sci_Name", "Com_Name", "Confidence"];
const csvRows = [headers.join(',')];

data.forEach(d => {
csvRows.push(`${d.Date},${d.Time},"${d.Sci_Name}","${d.Com_Name}",${d.C
});
const blob = new Blob([csvRows.join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv' });
const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
const a = document.createElement('a');
a.setAttribute('href', url);
a.setAttribute('download', `BirdNET_Export_${getTodayStr()}.csv`);
a.click();
}

document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
const species = document.getElementById('compile-species').value;
const conf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('compile-conf').value);
if(!species) return alert('Select a species first');
const btn = document.getElementById('compile-btn');
const status = document.getElementById('compile-status');
btn.disabled = true;
btn.innerText = 'Compiling...';
status.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-green-400', 'text-red-400');
status.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
status.innerText = 'Stitching audio files via FFmpeg...';
let start_date = "";
let end_date = "";
const tf = document.getElementById('compiler-timeframe').value;
if (tf === 'custom') {
start_date = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
end_date = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
} else if (tf !== 'all') {
const d = new Date();
end_date = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
d.setDate(d.getDate() - parseInt(tf));
start_date = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
}
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},

body: JSON.stringify({
species: species,
min_conf: conf,
limit: 25,
start_date: start_date,
end_date: end_date
})
});
const data = await res.json();

if(data.success) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';
}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
lowpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);

isAudioSetup = true;
}
let liveAudioCtx, liveAnalyser, liveSource, liveDataArray;
let isLiveAudioSetup = false;
let liveAnimId;
const liveCanvas = document.getElementById('live-spectro-canvas');
const liveCtx = liveCanvas.getContext('2d');
function initLiveSpectrogram() {
if (isLiveAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
liveAudioCtx = new AudioContext();
liveAnalyser = liveAudioCtx.createAnalyser();
liveAnalyser.fftSize = 512;
const sbAudio = document.getElementById('sidebar-audio');
liveSource = liveAudioCtx.createMediaElementSource(sbAudio);
liveSource.connect(liveAnalyser);
liveAnalyser.connect(liveAudioCtx.destination);
liveDataArray = new Uint8Array(liveAnalyser.frequencyBinCount);
isLiveAudioSetup = true;
liveCanvas.width = liveCanvas.offsetWidth;
liveCanvas.height = liveCanvas.offsetHeight;
}
function drawLiveSpectrogram() {
if (!document.getElementById('sidebar-audio').paused) {
liveAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(drawLiveSpectrogram);
liveAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(liveDataArray);
const w = liveCanvas.width;
const h = liveCanvas.height;
const imgData = liveCtx.getImageData(1, 0, w - 1, h);
liveCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
const validBins = Math.floor(liveDataArray.length * 0.75);
const binHeight = h / validBins;
for (let i = 0; i < validBins; i++) {
const val = liveDataArray[i];
const r = val > 128 ? 255 : val * 2;
const g = val > 192 ? 255 : (val > 128 ? (val - 128) * 2 : 0);
const b = val > 200 ? 255 : (val < 128 ? val : 0);
liveCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
liveCtx.fillRect(w - 1, h - (i * binHeight), 1, binHeight);

}
} else {
cancelAnimationFrame(liveAnimId);
}
}

const pageId = Object.keys(pages)[0];
if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

}

function updatePlayhead() {
if (!modalAudioEl.paused && modalAudioEl.duration) {
const pct = (modalAudioEl.currentTime / modalAudioEl.duration) * 100;
document.getElementById('modal-progress').style.width = `${pct}%`;
document.getElementById('modal-playhead').style.left = `${pct}%`;
document.getElementById('modal-time').innerText = `${Math.floor(modalAu
animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(updatePlayhead);
}
}
document.getElementById('modal-play-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
initWebAudioAPI();
if (audioCtx && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();

if(modalAudioEl.paused) {
modalAudioEl.play();
document.getElementById('modal-icon-play').classList.add('hidden');
document.getElementById('modal-icon-pause').classList.remove('hidden');

animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(updatePlayhead);
} else {
modalAudioEl.pause();
document.getElementById('modal-icon-play').classList.remove('hidden');
document.getElementById('modal-icon-pause').classList.add('hidden');
cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);
}
});

document.getElementById('modal-progress-container').addEventListener('click', (
if(modalAudioEl.duration) {
const rect = e.target.getBoundingClientRect();
modalAudioEl.currentTime = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * mod
const pct = (modalAudioEl.currentTime / modalAudioEl.duration) * 100;
document.getElementById('modal-progress').style.width = `${pct}%`;
document.getElementById('modal-playhead').style.left = `${pct}%`;
}
});
modalAudioEl.addEventListener('ended', () => {
document.getElementById('modal-icon-play').classList.remove('hidden');
document.getElementById('modal-icon-pause').classList.add('hidden');
document.getElementById('modal-playhead').style.left = '0%';
document.getElementById('modal-progress').style.width = '0%';

}

iBadge.className = 'px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded bg-green-90

}
async function restartService(serviceName) {
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/services/restart', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({service: serviceName})
});
if(res.ok) {
alert(`${serviceName} restart initiated.`);
setTimeout(updateServiceStatus, 2000);
} else {
alert(`Failed to restart ${serviceName}.`);
}
} catch(e) {
alert("Error connecting to backend.");
}
}

}

#!/usr/bin/env python3

CONFIG_PATH = os.path.expanduser('~/BirdNET-Pi/birdnet.conf')
def get_config():
config = {}
if os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH):
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
for line in f:
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):

key, val = line.strip().split('=', 1)
config[key] = val.strip(' "\'\n')
return config
def update_config(updates):
if not os.path.exists(CONFIG_PATH): return False
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'r') as f:
lines = f.readlines()
with open(CONFIG_PATH, 'w') as f:
for line in lines:
updated = False
if '=' in line and not line.startswith('#'):
key = line.split('=', 1)[0].strip()
if key in updates:
f.write(f'{key}="{updates[key]}"\n')
updated = True
if not updated:
f.write(line)
return True

<script>
let dbData = [];
let configData = {};
let activeChart = null;
let isMetric = false;
let galleryCacheRecent = [];
let galleryCacheBest = [];
let currentBestSort = 'name';
window.currentDbExport = [];
let modalChart = null;
let audioCtx;
let trackNode;
let gainNode;
let highpassNode;
let lowpassNode;
let isAudioSetup = false;

Chart.defaults.font.family = "'Inter', sans-serif";
function parseCSV(text) {
try {
const lines = text.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
if (lines.length < 2) return [];

const parsed = [];
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const row = lines[i].split(',').map(c => c.replace(/['"]/g, '').tri
if (row.length >= 5) {
parsed.push({

Date: row[0], Time: row[1], Sci_Name: row[2] || '', Com_Nam
});
}
}
return parsed;
} catch (e) {
return [];
}
}

document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(el => {
el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(nav => nav.classList.rem
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(tab => tab.classList.

const target = e.currentTarget;
target.classList.add('active');
const tabId = target.getAttribute('data-tab');
document.getElementById(`tab-${tabId}`).classList.add('active');
document.getElementById('current-tab-title').innerText = target.innerTe

const filter = document.getElementById('global-date-filter');
if (['dashboard', 'analytics'].includes(tabId)) filter.classList.remove
else filter.classList.add('hidden');

if (tabId === 'analytics') switchAnalytics('acc');
if (tabId === 'gallery' && galleryCacheRecent.length === 0) fetchGaller
if (tabId === 'tools') populateConfigForm();
});
});

function switchTools(view) {
document.querySelectorAll('#tab-tools > div > .toggle-btn').forEach(b => b.
event.target.classList.add('active');

['config', 'services', 'species'].forEach(v => document.getElementById(`too
document.getElementById(`tools-${view}`).classList.remove('hidden');
if(view === 'services') updateServiceStatus();
}

function getTodayStr() {
const d = new Date();
return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${S
}
async function init() {
const loader = document.getElementById('loading-indicator');
loader.classList.remove('hidden');
try {

const res = await fetch('/api/detections');
const text = await res.text();
dbData = parseCSV(text);
window.currentDbExport = dbData;
const confRes = await fetch('/api/config');
configData = await confRes.json();
updateSystemStats();
setInterval(updateSystemStats, 10000);
setInterval(pollLog, 3000);

applyGlobalFilter();
updateCompilerSuggestions();
populateDatabaseFilter();
filterDatabase();
} catch (e) {
document.getElementById('dash-feed').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-40
} finally {
loader.classList.add('hidden');
}
}

document.getElementById('global-date-filter').addEventListener('change', applyG

function filterDataByDays(sourceData, daysStr) {
if (daysStr === 'all' || sourceData.length === 0) return [...sourceData];
if (daysStr === 'today') return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date === getTodayS
if (daysStr === 'custom') {
const start = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
const end = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
if(!start || !end) return [...sourceData];
return sourceData.filter(d => d.Date >= start && d.Date <= end);
}
const latestDate = new Date(sourceData[0].Date);
const cutoff = new Date(latestDate);
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(daysStr));
return sourceData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
function applyGlobalFilter() {
if (dbData.length === 0) return;
const days = document.getElementById('global-date-filter').value;
const filtered = filterDataByDays(dbData, days);

renderDashboard(filtered);
if(document.getElementById('tab-analytics').classList.contains('active')) s
}

function filterDatabase() {
const sp = document.getElementById('db-filter-species').value;
const dStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-start').value;
const dEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-date-end').value;
const tStart = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-start').value;
const tEnd = document.getElementById('db-filter-time-end').value;
const minConf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('db-filter-conf').value)
let res = dbData;
if(sp !== 'all') res = res.filter(d => d.Com_Name === sp);
if(dStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Date >= dStart);
if(dEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Date <= dEnd);
if(tStart) res = res.filter(d => d.Time >= tStart);
if(tEnd) res = res.filter(d => d.Time <= tEnd);
if(minConf > 0) res = res.filter(d => d.Confidence >= minConf);
renderDatabaseTable(res);
window.currentDbExport = res;
}

function exportDatabaseCSV() {

});

a.setAttribute('href', url);
a.setAttribute('download', `BirdNET_Export_${getTodayStr()}.csv`);
a.click();
}

document.getElementById('compile-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
const species = document.getElementById('compile-species').value;
const conf = parseFloat(document.getElementById('compile-conf').value);
if(!species) return alert('Select a species first');
const btn = document.getElementById('compile-btn');
const status = document.getElementById('compile-status');
btn.disabled = true;
btn.innerText = 'Compiling...';
status.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-green-400', 'text-red-400');
status.classList.add('text-yellow-400');
status.innerText = 'Stitching audio files via FFmpeg...';
let start_date = "";
let end_date = "";
const tf = document.getElementById('compiler-timeframe').value;
if (tf === 'custom') {
start_date = document.getElementById('comp-date-start').value;
end_date = document.getElementById('comp-date-end').value;
} else if (tf !== 'all') {
const d = new Date();
end_date = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
d.setDate(d.getDate() - parseInt(tf));
start_date = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
}

try {
const res = await fetch('/api/compile', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({
species: species,
min_conf: conf,
limit: 25,
start_date: start_date,
end_date: end_date
})
});
const data = await res.json();

if(data.success) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-green-400');
status.innerHTML = `Successfully mixed ${data.count} recordings! <a
} else {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = data.error || 'Compilation failed.';
}
} catch(e) {
status.classList.replace('text-yellow-400', 'text-red-400');
status.innerText = 'Server error during compilation.';
} finally {
btn.disabled = false;
btn.innerText = 'Compile Audio';
}
});
const modalAudioEl = document.getElementById('modal-audio');
function initWebAudioAPI() {
if (isAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
audioCtx = new AudioContext();
trackNode = audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(modalAudioEl);
highpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
highpassNode.type = 'highpass';
highpassNode.frequency.value = 0;
lowpassNode = audioCtx.createBiquadFilter();
lowpassNode.type = 'lowpass';
lowpassNode.frequency.value = 24000;
gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();

trackNode.connect(highpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(lowpassNode);
highpassNode.connect(gainNode);
gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);
isAudioSetup = true;
}
let liveAudioCtx, liveAnalyser, liveSource, liveDataArray;
let isLiveAudioSetup = false;
let liveAnimId;
const liveCanvas = document.getElementById('live-spectro-canvas');
const liveCtx = liveCanvas.getContext('2d');
function initLiveSpectrogram() {
if (isLiveAudioSetup) return;
const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
liveAudioCtx = new AudioContext();
liveAnalyser = liveAudioCtx.createAnalyser();
liveAnalyser.fftSize = 512;
const sbAudio = document.getElementById('sidebar-audio');
liveSource = liveAudioCtx.createMediaElementSource(sbAudio);
liveSource.connect(liveAnalyser);
liveAnalyser.connect(liveAudioCtx.destination);
liveDataArray = new Uint8Array(liveAnalyser.frequencyBinCount);
isLiveAudioSetup = true;
liveCanvas.width = liveCanvas.offsetWidth;
liveCanvas.height = liveCanvas.offsetHeight;
}
function drawLiveSpectrogram() {
if (!document.getElementById('sidebar-audio').paused) {
liveAnimId = requestAnimationFrame(drawLiveSpectrogram);
liveAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(liveDataArray);
const w = liveCanvas.width;
const h = liveCanvas.height;
const imgData = liveCtx.getImageData(1, 0, w - 1, h);
liveCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
const validBins = Math.floor(liveDataArray.length * 0.75);
const binHeight = h / validBins;
for (let i = 0; i < validBins; i++) {
const val = liveDataArray[i];
const r = val > 128 ? 255 : val * 2;

const g = val > 192 ? 255 : (val > 128 ? (val - 128) * 2 : 0);
const b = val > 200 ? 255 : (val < 128 ? val : 0);
liveCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
liveCtx.fillRect(w - 1, h - (i * binHeight), 1, binHeight);
}
} else {
cancelAnimationFrame(liveAnimId);
}
}

async function fetchBirdImage(species) {
try {
const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&titles=${e
const res = await fetch(url);
const data = await res.json();
const pages = data.query.pages;
const pageId = Object.keys(pages)[0];
if (pageId !== "-1" && pages[pageId].thumbnail) return pages[pageId].th
} catch (e) {}
return null;
}

imgEl.classList.add('hidden');

function drawModalHistoryChart(species, days = '30') {
const ctx = document.getElementById('modal-history-chart').getContext('2d')
if (modalChart) modalChart.destroy();
let spData = dbData.filter(d => d.Com_Name === species);
if (days !== 'all') {
const cutoff = new Date();
cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - parseInt(days));
spData = spData.filter(d => new Date(d.Date) >= cutoff);
}
const daily = {};
spData.forEach(d => daily[d.Date] = (daily[d.Date] || 0) + 1);
const labels = []; const data = [];
if (days === 'all' && spData.length > 0) {
const allDates = [...new Set(spData.map(d => d.Date))].sort();
if(allDates.length > 0) {
let curr = new Date(allDates[0]);
const end = new Date();
while (curr <= end) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));
data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}
} else {
let curr = new Date();
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() - parseInt(days === 'all' ? 30 : days));
while (curr <= new Date()) {
const dStr = curr.toISOString().split('T')[0];
labels.push(dStr.substring(5));

data.push(daily[dStr] || 0);
curr.setDate(curr.getDate() + 1);
}
}

modalChart = new Chart(ctx, {
type: 'line',
data: {
labels: labels,
datasets: [{
data: data,
borderColor: '#ef4444',
backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
fill: true,
tension: 0.4,
pointRadius: 0,
borderWidth: 2
}]
},
options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { leg
});
}

}

}

btn.classList.remove('bg-red-600', 'text-white');
dot.classList.replace('bg-[#4ade80]', 'bg-slate-500');

async function updateServiceStatus() {
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/services');
const data = await res.json();

const aBadge = document.getElementById('status-analysis');
if (data['birdnet_analysis.service']) {
aBadge.innerText = 'RUNNING';
aBadge.className = 'px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded bg-green-90
} else {
aBadge.innerText = 'STOPPED';
aBadge.className = 'px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded bg-red-900
}

const iBadge = document.getElementById('status-icecast');
if (data['icecast2.service']) {
iBadge.innerText = 'RUNNING';
iBadge.className = 'px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded bg-green-90
} else {
iBadge.innerText = 'STOPPED';
iBadge.className = 'px-2 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded bg-red-900
}
} catch(e) {}
}
async function restartService(serviceName) {
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/services/restart', {
method: 'POST',
headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
body: JSON.stringify({service: serviceName})
});
if(res.ok) {
alert(`${serviceName} restart initiated.`);
setTimeout(updateServiceStatus, 2000);
} else {
alert(`Failed to restart ${serviceName}.`);
}
} catch(e) {
alert("Error connecting to backend.");
}
}

async function pollLog() {
if(!document.getElementById('tab-log').classList.contains('active')) return
try {
const res = await fetch('/api/log');
const text = await res.text();
const out = document.getElementById('log-output');
if(out.innerText !== text) { out.innerText = text; out.scrollTop = out.
} catch(e) {}
}

User prompt: Any way I could connect you to like VScode (or another app I'm not picky) through
some api so when you code you're pushing changes to files on some kind of shared filesystem (or
at least something thats easily accessible/saveable for me, plus should save tokens right since you
dont need to output the entire file each time, can just alter / rewrite / whatever)
Response: Yes, absolutely! The workflow you are describing—where an AI assistant directly reads,
writes, and patches code files on your local machine or a shared filesystem instead of you having
to manually copy-paste massive blocks of text—is the gold standard for software development. It
also drastically cuts down on token usage because you only transmit diffs or specific file
modifications.
To achieve this, you have a few great pathways depending on how tightly you want to integrate:

1. Dedicated AI Code Editors & Extensions (The Easiest Route)
Instead of building a custom bridge yourself, tools designed specifically for this workflow already
exist:
• VS Code Extensions (like Gemini Code Assist or Roo Code / Cline): Extensions like
Cline or Roo Code allow you to plug in a Gemini API key directly inside VS Code. They grant
the AI "workspace access," meaning the model can natively read your directory tree, locate
index.html or birdnet_core.py, and apply surgical diffs or file rewrites right inside your
editor with your approval.
• Cursor / Windsurf: These are dedicated IDE forks of VS Code built from the ground up for
agentic coding. They let models read whole workspaces, modify files directly, and run
terminal commands.

2. Building a Lightweight Local Bridge (The DIY Route)
If you want to keep collaborating through a chat interface like this or a custom script, you can build
a tiny local FastAPI or Flask server on your Raspberry Pi that exposes endpoints like /api/savefile.

• How it works: A small script runs on your Pi. When we code together, I can generate a
structured JSON payload containing just the line changes or updated blocks. Your local script
receives it and writes it directly to ~/BirdNET-Pi/index.html.
• Token efficiency: This approach drops token usage significantly because you aren't sending
1,000 lines of unchanged HTML back and forth on every prompt.
If you're writing code locally on the Pi right now, looking into an extension like Cline in VS Code
with a Gemini API key connected is usually the fastest way to get direct file-writing capabilities
without losing conversational flexibility.

User prompt: Ok I setup gemini code assist standard in vscode and made a new project. What's
the best way to import the files and chat history into there

Response: Importing Your Files
Since you have your running files (birdnet_core.py and index.html) ready, moving them into
your new VS Code workspace is straightforward:
�. Open the Project Folder in VS Code: Go to File > Open Folder... and select the local
directory where you want to house your project.
2. Drop the Files In: Simply drag and drop your existing birdnet_core.py and index.html
files directly from your file explorer into the root of the file tree on the left side of VS Code.
3. Verify Gemini Code Assist: Press Ctrl + Shift + P (or Cmd + Shift + P on macOS) to
open the command palette, type Gemini: Enable Code Assist (or check the extension
panel on the right/sidebar) to ensure it's active and successfully authenticated for your
workspace.

Handling Chat History
IDE-native chat panels (like Gemini Code Assist inside VS Code) operate within a fresh workspace
session and do not automatically import historical chat logs from external web browser interfaces.
To give your new VS Code environment instant context without needing to re-explain past
debugging trials, the cleanest method is to drop your project handover file directly into your
workspace:
• Create a new file in your project root named HANDOVER.md.
• Paste the summary or brain-dump of your project state, architecture, and design rules into it.
• Whenever you open a chat session with Gemini Code Assist in VS Code, you can reference
it immediately by typing @HANDOVER.md or telling the assistant to read it first. This instantly
initializes the AI with the exact history, constraints, and past decisions we've established.
